\begin{document}
\section*{T3 candidate UV and EFT1 scalar RGEs}
\textbf{Provenance:} saved 16-model operator registry and candidate one-loop RGBeta UV/EFT1 exports. Source beta expressions are algebraically parsed from saved InputForm and typeset in paper coupling notation. No implicit Matchete rescaling.
\textbf{Status:} candidate tensor basis. General scalar RGBeta--Matchete mapping, quadratic mass-basis matching and full sequential Wilson matching are not certified.
\textit{Direct transcription of generated candidate one-loop RGBeta exports; no new symbolic RGE computation is performed.}
\bigskip
\section*{Conventions and completeness}
All expressions below are parsed from saved native RGBeta InputForm and typeset in mathematical notation without changing field convention or polynomial normalisation:
\[16\pi^2\frac{d X}{d\ln\mu}=\beta_X^{(1)}.\]
\textbf{UV}: SM+$F$+$S_1$+$S_2$. \textbf{EFT1}: fermion integrated out, both scalars retained. These are the saved candidate renormalisable beta functions; no Weinberg or $C_{12}$ Wilson evolution is inferred from them.
\textbf{Scope}: all scalar quartics, scalar mass squares and holomorphic bilinears, including the original $\lambda_5$.
\section*{Scalar coupling notation}
The original topology interaction remains $\lambda_5$. Original scalar and portal interactions retain the notation of the earlier analytical reports. Additional quartic structures use $\lambda_7$--$\lambda_{12}$ where these symbols already exist, followed by $\lambda_{13},\lambda_{14},\ldots$ for newly named operators. All labels refer to RGBeta potential coefficients in the saved source convention; the Matchete--RGBeta mapping is not fully certified.
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.21\linewidth}p{0.46\linewidth}p{0.23\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
Symbol & Scalar field structure & Models \\
\midrule
\endhead
$\lambda_{7}$ & $\left(H^\dagger\right)^{2}\, \left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{2}$ & B-1, C-1 \\
$\lambda_{8}$ & $\left(H^\dagger\right)^{2}\, S_2^{2}$ & B-1, C-1 \\
$\lambda_{9}$ & $\left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{2}\, \left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{2}$ & B-1, C-1 \\
$\lambda_{10}$ & $S_1^\dagger\, S_2\, \left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{2}$ & B-1, C-1 \\
$\lambda_{11}$ & $S_1\, \left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{2}\, S_2^\dagger$ & B-1, C-1 \\
$\lambda_{12}$ & $H\, H^\dagger\, S_1^\dagger\, S_2^\dagger$ & B-1, C-1 \\
$\lambda_{13}$ & $\left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{4}$ & A-2, D-2, E-2 \\
$\lambda_{14}$ & $S_2\, \left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{3}$ & A-2, D-2, E-2 \\
$\lambda_{15}$ & $\left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{2}\, S_2\, S_2^\dagger$ & A+0, D+0, E+0 \\
$\lambda_{16}$ & $\left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{2}\, S_2\, S_2^\dagger$ & E+0 \\
$\lambda_{17}$ & $\left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{4}$ & A+0, D+0, E+0 \\
$\lambda_{18}$ & $S_1\, S_1^\dagger\, \left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{2}$ & A-2, D-2, E-2 \\
$\lambda_{19}$ & $S_1\, S_1^\dagger\, \left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{2}$ & E-2 \\
$\lambda_{20}$ & $S_1\, \left(S_1^\dagger\right)^{3}$ & A+0, D+0, E+0 \\
$\lambda_{21}$ & $\left(H^\dagger\right)^{2}\, \left(S_2^\dagger\right)^{2}$ & B-3, C-3 \\
$\lambda_{22}$ & $\left(H^\dagger\right)^{2}\, S_1^\dagger\, S_2^\dagger$ & A-2, D-2, E-2 \\

For distinct SU(2) contractions with the same field content, numbered superscripts/index labels remain independent. These symbols denote model-dependent invariant tensors, not universal Clebsch coefficients.
